But... well, most days lately I just end up sitting at the kitchen table. It just seems like... like the color has gone out of everything. I try to look at my old watercolor books, but... they don't interest me right now. I just haven't felt like picking up a book lately. Um... like lecture plans, or travel notes. Even just the regular household paperwork. I just... I can't seem to organize myself the way I used to. So... yes, I guess it does.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that your feelings of being foggy and disinterested are mild, or are they quite overwhelming most of the time?\n\nPatient: I don't know if overwhelming is the right word. Most of the time.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that most days you have little to no interest in doing things, or is it more like you have some interest but just can't seem to get started?\n\nPatient: I guess... it's more like little to no interest.
